exempt pdfcer-core variant pdfcer_core::edit::EncryptError::RedactionPending -- FOUND ON THIS GATE'S FIRST REAL RUN, NOT DISMISSED, and standing here only until an ENGINE_BACKLOG.md row that another track owns is placed (row text reported to the operator 2026-09-05). THE FINDING: crates/pdfcer-gui/src/protect/mod.rs:510 flattens EncryptError arm by arm into EngineRefusal and has NO arm for this variant, so the engine's Pass 250.3 refusal - "a deferred redaction is staged, so the un-redacted content is still live" - is delivered to the operator as EngineRefusal::Write, a WRITER error. Nothing leaks: the catch-all carries the engine's own message verbatim. But EngineRefusal's own doc comment claims it "turns a new engine variant into a compile error here rather than a silent fall-through to a catch-all", and that claim is FALSE for a #[non_exhaustive] enum - the catch-all arm is mandatory, so the compiler can never object and the stated mechanism has never been able to fire. DELETE THIS LINE the day the arm is added or the row is filed.
exempt pdfcer-render field pdfcer_render::font::RenderPolicy::stroke_display -- RenderPolicy is the interpreter's INTERNAL derived bundle, not a shell-facing type: every one of its fields' own doc comments reads "See RenderOptions::<the same name>", and the engine builds a RenderPolicy from the RenderOptions a caller hands it. This shell sets RenderOptions::stroke_display (View > Display > Line weights, O137) and constructs no RenderPolicy anywhere in the workspace. Exempted PER FIELD rather than wildcarded for the whole type, deliberately: every future RenderPolicy field mirrors a RenderOptions field this shell probably DOES want, so each one should cost one line and one look.
